    def test_single_manual_review_does_not_abort_report(monkeypatch):

contract_text = '合作开发协议书\n以下主体、项目、金额及其他信息均为虚构测试数据，仅用于合同审查系统测试。\n甲方：南京智算科技有限公司（虚构）\n统一社会信用代码：91320100TEST202601\n乙方：苏州云核软件有限公司（虚构）\n统一社...，双方各持两份。\n自双方盖章之日起生效。\n甲方：\n南京智算科技有限公司（虚构）\n法定代表人/授权代表：\n陈宇航\n乙方：\n苏州云核软件有限公司（虚构）\n法定代表人/授权代表：\n赵文博\n签署日期：\n2026年8月24日'
contract_type = 'technology', include_low_risk = True, use_llm = False
profile = UserRiskProfile(user_identity='甲方/合作开发方', contract_scenario='合作开发', risk_appetite='标准', bargaining_position='均衡方', jurisdiction='北京')
source_input_diagnostics = None

    def review_contract_v2(
        contract_text: str,
        contract_type: Optional[str] = "house_lease",
        *,
        include_low_risk: bool = True,
        use_llm: bool = True,
        profile: Optional[UserRiskProfile] = None,
        source_input_diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """按第二版审查清单审查合同。
    
        支持用户风险画像（UserRiskProfile）驱动审查输出：
        - 风险等级个性化（保守/标准/激进）
        - 修改建议语气动态调整
        - 审查点优先激活/过滤
        """
    
        started_at = time.perf_counter()
        contract_review_trace_id = f"CR-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
    
        normalized_type = (contract_type or "house_lease").strip().lower()
        try:
            type_meta = get_contract_meta(contract_type)
        except UnsupportedContractTypeError:
            type_meta = None
        llm_status = get_llm_review_status()
        if not use_llm:
            global_fallback_reason = "本次审查未启用大模型语义复核，使用规则判定"
        elif not llm_status.get("available"):
            global_fallback_reason = llm_status.get("message", "")
        else:
            global_fallback_reason = ""
    
        if not type_meta:
            profile = _request_scoped_profile(profile, normalized_type)
            return {
                "success": False,
                "contract_type": normalized_type,
                "contract_type_name": "未支持的合同类型",
                "message": f"审查第二版当前支持{'、'.join(supported_contract_display_names())}；其他合同类型应按同一审查清单结构迁移。",
                "contract_review_trace_id": contract_review_trace_id,
                "items": [],
                "summary": {"高": 0, "中": 0, "低": 0},
                "risk_finding_summary": {"重大风险": 0, "中等风险": 0, "低风险": 0, "人工复核": 0},
                "final_overview_summary": {"major_risk": 0, "medium_risk": 0, "low_risk": 0, "priority_action": 0, "recommended_action": 0, "optional_action": 0, "manual_review": 0},
                "coverage_summary": {},
                "issue_type_summary": {},
                "judge_source_summary": {},
                "llm_enabled": use_llm,
                "llm_status": llm_status,
                "global_fallback_reason": global_fallback_reason,
                "report_notice": REPORT_FORMAT_NOTICE,
                "result": "",
                "review_results_4_0": [],
                "risk_findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
                "contract_context": build_contract_context(normalized_type, {}, profile, contract_text=contract_text).to_public_dict(),
                "overall_assessment": {},
                "legal_reference_issues": [],
                "legal_citation_claims": [],
                "legal_authority_validation": {},
                "benchmark_metrics": {},
                "release_quality_gate": {"status": "BLOCKED", "failures": [{"requirement": "Supported Contract Type", "failure_stage": "Parser Validation", "root_cause": "Unsupported contract type.", "responsible_function": "get_contract_meta", "current_status": "FAIL"}]},
                "quality4_gate": {"status": "BLOCKED", "failures": [{"requirement": "Supported Contract Type", "failure_stage": "Parser Validation", "root_cause": "Unsupported contract type.", "fix_owner": "Contract Registry"}]},
                "architecture_status": _architecture_status(),
                "system_metadata": _system_metadata(llm_status),
                "observability": {"total_latency_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - started_at) * 1000, 2)},
                "profile": profile.to_dict(),
            }
    
        # The classifier, clause parser, SEARCH_AUDIT and rule engine must consume
        # one immutable normalized full-text value.  Naming the stage inputs
        # separately keeps their lineage observable without permitting a summary or
        # structured field map to replace the contract body.
        review_text = strip_non_contract_tail(contract_text)
        classifier_input = review_text
        clause_extraction_input = review_text
        rule_engine_input = review_text
        search_audit_input = review_text
        clauses = parse_contract_clauses(clause_extraction_input)
        clause_extraction_output = "\n".join(
            str(clause.raw_text or clause.text or "")
            for clause in clauses
            if str(clause.raw_text or clause.text or "").strip()
        )
        input_integrity = build_input_integrity_diagnostics(
            extracted_text=contract_text,
            normalized_text=review_text,
            classifier_input=classifier_input,
            clause_extraction_input=clause_extraction_input,
            rule_engine_input=rule_engine_input,
            search_audit_input=search_audit_input,
            clause_extraction_output=clause_extraction_output,
            source_diagnostics=source_input_diagnostics,
        )
        if input_integrity["status"] == "BLOCKED":
            profile = _request_scoped_profile(profile, type_meta.response_key)
            failures = [
                {
                    "requirement": row.get("code"),
                    "failure_stage": row.get("stage"),
                    "root_cause": row.get("message"),
                    "responsible_function": "build_input_integrity_diagnostics",
                    "entity_type": "document",
                    "entity_id": row.get("anchor") or "contract_package/main",
                    "severity": "fatal",
                    "current_status": "FAIL",
                }
                for row in input_integrity.get("failures") or []
            ]
            fatal_gate = {
                "status": "FATAL",
                "fatal": True,
                "failures": failures,
                "fatal_rule_ids": [str(row.get("requirement") or "") for row in failures],
                "gate_results": {"INPUT_TEXT_LINEAGE_GATE": "FAIL"},
            }
            return {
                "success": False,
                "contract_type": type_meta.response_key,
                "selected_contract_type": type_meta.response_key,
                "contract_type_name": type_meta.display_name,
                "contract_review_trace_id": contract_review_trace_id,
                "message": input_integrity_user_message(input_integrity),
                "items": [],
                "all_items_count": 0,
                "summary": {"高": 0, "中": 0, "低": 0},
                "risk_finding_summary": {"重大风险": 0, "中等风险": 0, "低风险": 0, "人工复核": 0},
                "final_overview_summary": {"major_risk": 0, "medium_risk": 0, "low_risk": 0, "priority_action": 0, "recommended_action": 0, "optional_action": 0, "manual_review": 0},
                "coverage_summary": {},
                "issue_type_summary": {},
                "judge_source_summary": {},
                "llm_enabled": use_llm,
                "llm_status": llm_status,
                "global_fallback_reason": global_fallback_reason,
                "report_notice": "",
                "result": "合同输入链路完整性校验未通过，正式报告未生成。",
                "input_integrity": input_integrity,
                "fatal_report_generation_gate": fatal_gate,
                "report_generation_decision": {
                    "status": "STOPPED_FATAL",
                    "report_generated": False,
                    "fatal_rule_ids": fatal_gate["fatal_rule_ids"],
                    "quality_status": "BLOCKED",
                    "non_fatal_quality_rule_ids": [],
                },
                "release_quality_gate": {"status": "BLOCKED", "failures": failures},
                "quality4_gate": {"status": "BLOCKED", "failures": failures},
                "system_metadata": _system_metadata(llm_status),
                "observability": {"input_integrity": input_integrity},
                "profile": profile.to_dict(),
            }
    
        begin_payment_analysis_session(classifier_input)
        begin_technology_classification_session(classifier_input)
        technology_classification = classify_technology_transaction(classifier_input)
        integrity = document_integrity(review_text, clauses).to_public_dict()
        fatal_report_generation_gate = _fatal_report_generation_gate(review_text, clauses, integrity)
        user_selected_type = type_meta.response_key
        domain_selection_resolution, domain_resolution, resolved_type = resolve_domain_selection(
            user_selected_type,
            review_text,
            clauses,
            technology_classification=technology_classification,
        )
        type_meta = get_contract_meta(resolved_type)
        profile = _request_scoped_profile(profile, type_meta.response_key)
        technology_classification = technology_classification if type_meta.key.value == "technology" else {}
        previous_context_role = "LESSEE" if user_selected_type == "house_lease" else "BUYER" if user_selected_type == "sale" else ""
        perspective_resolution = resolve_perspective(
            profile.user_identity,
            domain_resolution.contract_family,
            previous_context_role=previous_context_role,
            contract_subtype=str(
                technology_classification.get("tertiary_subtype")
                or technology_classification.get("primary_subtype")
                or domain_resolution.contract_subtype
            ),
            contract_text=review_text,
        )
        if type_meta.key.value == "sale" and domain_resolution.subject_matter == "REAL_ESTATE":
            # Registered-property schedules commonly follow signature blocks and remain contractual evidence.
            # ``strip_non_contract_tail`` retains contractual schedules; keep using
            # the immutable normalized input instead of silently switching only the
            # rule/parser stage back to a different source value.
            clauses = parse_contract_clauses(rule_engine_input)
            integrity = document_integrity(rule_engine_input, clauses).to_public_dict()
            domain_resolution = resolve_contract_domain(type_meta.key.value, rule_engine_input, clauses)
        generic_fact_graph = extract_contract_fact_graph(clauses)
        patent_transfer_analysis = (
            analyze_patent_transfer(review_text)
            if technology_classification.get("tertiary_subtype") == "PATENT_RIGHT_TRANSFER"
            else None
        )
        if patent_transfer_analysis:
            add_patent_transfer_facts_to_graph(patent_transfer_analysis, clauses, generic_fact_graph)
        framework_supply = extract_framework_supply_profile(review_text, clauses) if type_meta.key.value == "sale" else None
        if framework_supply:
            add_framework_facts_to_graph(framework_supply, clauses, generic_fact_graph)
        real_estate_facts = None
        if type_meta.key.value == "sale" and domain_resolution.subject_matter == "REAL_ESTATE":
            real_estate_facts = extract_real_estate_sale_facts(review_text, clauses, generic_fact_graph)
        fact_registry_snapshot_id = _fact_registry_snapshot_id(generic_fact_graph)
        generic_fact_graph.freeze(fact_registry_snapshot_id)
        checklist = get_checklist_for_resolution(type_meta.key, domain_resolution)
        registry_debug = review_registry_debug(type_meta.key, domain_resolution)
        lease_facts = extract_house_lease_facts(review_text) if type_meta.key.value == "lease" else None
        contract_facts = lease_facts.to_public_dict() if lease_facts else generic_fact_graph.to_public_dict()
        contract_facts["fact_registry_snapshot_id"] = fact_registry_snapshot_id
        contract_facts["domain_resolution"] = domain_resolution.to_public_dict()
        contract_facts["domain_selection_resolution"] = domain_selection_resolution.to_public_dict()
        if framework_supply:
            contract_facts["framework_supply"] = framework_supply.to_public_dict()
        if real_estate_facts:
            contract_facts["real_estate"] = real_estate_facts.to_public_dict()
        if patent_transfer_analysis:
            contract_facts["patent_transfer"] = patent_transfer_analysis
        canonical_claims = build_canonical_claims(review_text, clauses, generic_fact_graph, domain_resolution.subject_matter)
        contract_facts["canonical_claims"] = canonical_claims_public(canonical_claims)
        product_documentation_profile = (
            build_product_documentation_profile(review_text, clauses, domain_resolution)
            if type_meta.key.value == "sale" and domain_resolution.subject_matter in {"GOODS", "EQUIPMENT"}
            else None
        )
        if product_documentation_profile:
            contract_facts["product_documentation_profile"] = product_documentation_profile.to_public_dict()
        contract_context = build_contract_context(type_meta.response_key, contract_facts, profile, contract_text=review_text, domain_resolution=domain_resolution).to_public_dict()
        contract_context["user_role"] = perspective_resolution.canonical_role.lower()
        contract_context["user_perspective"] = perspective_resolution.canonical_role.lower()
        contract_context["review_party_identity"] = perspective_resolution.review_party_identity
        contract_context["review_party_label"] = perspective_resolution.review_party_label
        contract_context["transaction_role"] = perspective_resolution.transaction_role
        contract_context["transaction_role_object"] = {
            "transaction_type": perspective_resolution.transaction_type,
            "review_party": perspective_resolution.review_party,
            "legal_role": perspective_resolution.legal_role,
            "contract_display_role": perspective_resolution.contract_display_role,
        }
        if technology_classification:
            primary_subtype = str(technology_classification.get("primary_subtype") or "")
            has_specific_technology_subtype = primary_subtype in {
                "COOPERATIVE_DEVELOPMENT",
                "ENTRUSTED_DEVELOPMENT",
                "TECHNOLOGY_TRANSFER",
                "TECHNOLOGY_LICENSE",
                "TECHNICAL_CONSULTING",
                "TECHNICAL_SERVICE",
            }
            canonical_subtype = str(
                primary_subtype
                if has_specific_technology_subtype
                else contract_context.get("contract_subtype") or primary_subtype
            )
            canonical_transaction = str(
                (technology_classification.get("transaction_structure") if has_specific_technology_subtype else None)
                or contract_context.get("transaction_structure")
                or domain_resolution.transaction_structure
            )
            category_by_subtype = {
                "COOPERATIVE_DEVELOPMENT": "TECHNOLOGY_DEVELOPMENT",
                "ENTRUSTED_DEVELOPMENT": "TECHNOLOGY_DEVELOPMENT",
                "TECHNOLOGY_TRANSFER": "TECHNOLOGY_TRANSFER",
                "TECHNOLOGY_LICENSE": "TECHNOLOGY_LICENSE",
                "TECHNICAL_CONSULTING": "TECHNICAL_CONSULTING",
                "TECHNICAL_SERVICE": "TECHNICAL_SERVICE",
            }
            contract_context["canonical_classification"] = {
                "contract_family": str(technology_classification.get("contract_family") or "TECHNOLOGY"),
                "contract_category": str(
                    technology_classification.get("contract_category")
                    or category_by_subtype.get(primary_subtype)
                    or primary_subtype
                    or "GENERAL_TECHNOLOGY"
                ),
                "contract_subtype": canonical_subtype,
                "transaction_structure": canonical_transaction,
                "scenario": str(technology_classification.get("scenario_label") or ""),
                "classification_evidence": list(technology_classification.get("classification_evidence") or []),
            }
            if has_specific_technology_subtype:
                contract_context["contract_subtype"] = canonical_subtype
                contract_context["transaction_structure"] = canonical_transaction
            if technology_classification.get("tertiary_subtype") in {"TECHNICAL_SECRET_TRANSFER", "TECHNICAL_SECRET_LICENSE"}:
                # build_contract_context starts from the broad technology family;
                # keep the resolved transaction subject when projecting the report.
                contract_context["subject_matter"] = "TECHNICAL_SECRET"
                contract_context["primary_subject"] = "TECHNICAL_SECRET"
            elif technology_classification.get("tertiary_subtype") == "PATENT_APPLICATION_RIGHT_TRANSFER":
                contract_context["subject_matter"] = "PATENT_APPLICATION_RIGHT"
                contract_context["primary_subject"] = "PATENT_APPLICATION_RIGHT"
        if perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "PATENT_TRANSFEREE":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "patent_transferor"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "PATENT_TRANSFEROR":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "patent_transferee"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "PATENT_LICENSEE":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "patent_licensor"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "PATENT_LICENSOR":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "patent_licensee"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "COOPERATIVE_DEVELOPER":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "cooperative_developer"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "COMMISSIONING_PARTY":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "development_contractor"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "DEVELOPMENT_CONTRACTOR":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "commissioning_party"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "CONSULTING_CLIENT":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "technical_consultant"
        elif perspective_resolution.canonical_role == "TECHNICAL_CONSULTANT":
            contract_context["counterparty_role"] = "consulting_client"
        contract_context["perspective_resolution"] = perspective_resolution.to_public_dict()

            applicable_default_law=DEFAULT_LAW_VERSION,

    def test_future_development_warranty_does_not_trigger_existing_ip_proof_attachments():
        text = """
        软件委托开发合同。甲方委托乙方开发新的业务系统。
        开发成果和源代码交付甲方，乙方保证开发成果不侵犯第三方知识产权。
        """
        output = review_contract_v2(
            text,

    def test_fixed_price_table_does_not_create_unilateral_price_finding():
        contract = """买卖合同
    甲方（出卖人）：甲公司
    乙方（买受人）：乙公司
    一、货物及价款
    产品A，单价420元，数量500吨。合同价格固定，任何价格变更须经双方书面协商一致。
    二、交付
    甲方应于2026年9月1日前交付至乙方仓库。

    def test_current_secret_license_full_regression(contract_text: str, review: dict):
        classification = classify_technology_transaction(contract_text)
        payment = analyze_payment_schedule(contract_text)
        by_id = {row["item_id"]: row for row in review["items"]}
    
        assert classification["primary_subtype"] == "TECHNOLOGY_LICENSE"
        assert classification["tertiary_subtype"] == "TECHNICAL_SECRET_LICENSE"
        assert classification["primary_contract_type"] == "technology_license_technical_secret"
        assert classification["scenario"] == "TECHNICAL_SECRET_LICENSE"
        assert {"technical_service", "technical_guidance", "acceptance", "technical_secret"} <= set(
            classification["sub_transactions"]
        )
    
        assert payment["status"] == "INCONSISTENT"
        assert payment["contract_total"] == 2_400_000
        assert payment["percentage_total"] == 120
        assert payment["amount_total"] == 2_880_000
        assert payment["amount_delta"] == 480_000
